Use when working with .md files in Obsidian, or when the user mentions wikilinks, callouts, frontmatter, tags, embeds, or Obsidian notes.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. baseline 상위 시드 중 graph-walk 를 실행할 최대 개수 — CPU 상한 (plan §0\n79\t * Reality Anchor #1). 각 시드 walk = 재귀 쿼리 1회. 시드 수 × walk 비용을\n80\t * bound. 5 = 정상 topK(3)·MAX(10) 대비 충분(중복 확장은 dedup 으로 흡수).\n81\t */\n82\texport const GRAPH_SEED_WALK_LIMIT = 5;\n83\t\n84\t/\*\*\n85\t * 공개 계약 query 상한 — 기존 500 **유지** (`/api/search` routes.ts:61 정합,\n86\t * DoS/임베딩 비용 가드). MASTER_PLAN WS-4 4c \"query 500자 천장 처리\" 는\n87\t * 공개 계약 분리가 아닌 **측정 경로 한정 우회**(debug 플래그 결합 상향)로\n88\t * 처분 — 보수적 택1.\n89\t */\n90\texport const GRAPH_QUERY_PUBLIC_MAX_LENGTH = 500;\n91\t\n92\t/**\n93\t * debug(측정) 플래그 결합 시 query 절대 상한 — golden Q-004(583자, queryBody\n94\t * 정화로도 미회복 → 종전 영구 제외)와 정화 전 최장 golden(Q-015 원문 922자)\n95\t * 을 여유 있게 커버. bge-m3 입력 한도(8192 tokens) 대비 안전 + rate-limit\n96\t * (60/min per IP) 병행으로 남용 표면 bounded. 무제한이 아닌 절대 상한 유지\n97\t * (Reality Anchor — 측정 경로도 계약은 있다).\n98\t */\n99\texport const GRAPH_QUERY_DEBUG_MAX_LENGTH = 2000;\n100\t\n101\tconst GraphSearchBodySchema = z\n102\t .object({\n103\t examId: z.string().min(1),\n104\t query: z.string().min(1).max(GRAPH_QUERY_DEBUG_MAX_LENGTH),\n105\t topK: z.number().int().min(1).max(MAX_RESULT_TOP_K).default(DEFAULT_RESULT_TOP_K),\n106\t /**\n107\t * graph-walk maxDepth. HTTP 계약 상한 = 엔진 hard ceiling\n108\t * `MAX_ALLOWED_DEPTH`(D-2=4). 초과 시 silent clamp 아닌 **400 정직 거부**\n109\t * (S5-5 Pass3 Minor-1). 엔진 clampInt 는 방어선으로 잔존(이중 방어).\n110\t */\n111\t maxDepth: z.number().int().min(1).max(MAX_ALLOWED_DEPTH).optional(),\n112\t /** graph-walk resultCap. HTTP 상한 = 엔진 `MAX_ALLOWED_RESULT_CAP`. \*/\n113\t resultCap: z.number().int().min(1).max(MAX_ALLOWED_RESULT_CAP).optional(),\n114\t /**\n115\t * 진단·측정 플래그 (MASTER_PLAN WS-4 4c, 기본 off). true 시:\n116\t * (a) `graphExpansion.expandedNodes` 로 graph 확장 전체집합 surface\n117\t * — 랭크미달 vs 미도달 판별(Binary Gate G-WS4 ③) 재료.\n118\t * (b) query 상한 500 → `GRAPH_QUERY_DEBUG_MAX_LENGTH` 상향\n119\t * — golden Q-004(583자) 측정 포함(G-WS4 ④), 공개 계약 500 불변.\n120\t * 응답 계약 additive — 기존 필드 전부 불변.\n121\t */\n122\t debug: z.boolean().default(false),\n123\t /\*\*\n124\t * Phase 1-D 비교군 모드 (lexical-fusion-phase1d.plan.md rev3).\n125\t * 'graph'(default) = 기존 계약 byte-동치. 'lexical' = graph-walk 무접촉,\n126\t * vector pool(10) 을 lexical 신호로 ε-양자화 재정렬(주입 없음 — M-2).\n127\t * 측정 전용: mode='lexical' 은 debug=true 필수(superRefine).\n128\t */\n129\t mode: z.enum(['graph', 'lexical']).default('graph'),\n130\t /**\n131\t * lexical ε-band 폭 (감도 스윕 M-5·동치 테스트 m-4 전용). graph 경로는\n132\t * eps 미독(무시 — rev3 m-N2). 기본 0.03(provenance: F2 실측 Δ0.02 유래 —\n133\t * 측정셋 적합·일반화 근거 아님, plan §0-7).\n134\t */\n135\t eps: z.number().min(0).max(0.1).default(0.03),\n136\t })\n137\t .superRefine((data, ctx) => {\n138\t // 공개 계약(debug 미사용) = 종전 그대로 500 상한. debug=true 만 측정\n139\t // 경로 한정 우회 — silent 완화 아닌 명시 opt-in.\n140\t if (!data.debug && data.query.length > GRAPH_QUERY_PUBLIC_MAX_LENGTH) {\n141\t ctx.addIssue({\n142\t code: z.ZodIssueCode.too_big,\n143\t maximum: GRAPH_QUERY_PUBLIC_MAX_LENGTH,\n144\t type: 'string',\n145\t inclusive: true,\n146\t path: ['query'],\n147\t message: `query length > ${GRAPH_QUERY_PUBLIC_MAX_LENGTH} (public contract; debug flag required)`,\n148\t });\n149\t }\n150\t // mode='lexical' = 측정 전용 명시 게이트 (plan rev3 m-3 — 공개 남용 표면 차단).\n151\t if (data.mode === 'lexical' && !data.debug) {\n152\t ctx.addIssue({\n153\t code: z.ZodIssueCode.custom,\n154\t path: ['mode'],\n155\t message: `mode='lexical' requires debug=true (measurement-only path)`,\n156\t });\n157\t }\n158\t });\n159\t\n160\t/**\n161\t * debug=true 한정 확장 노드 진단 뷰 — `GraphWalkNode` 에서 description 만\n162\t * 제외(법령 본문 텍스트 payload 비대 차단; 진단은 id/depth/weight 로 충분).\n163\t */\n164\texport interface GraphExpansionDebugNode {\n165\t readonly id: string;\n166\t /\*\* 시드로부터 hop 거리 (시드별 최단 — 다중 시드 재도달은 첫 도달분 1회). */\n167\t readonly depth: number;\n168\t readonly type: string;\n169\t readonly name: string;\n170\t readonly truthWeight: number;\n171\t readonly pageRef: string | null;\n172\t}\n173\t\n174\tinterface GraphExpansionMeta {\n175\t readonly applied: boolean;\n176\t /** 미적용 사유 (시드 0건 등) — applied=false 일 때만. \*/\n177\t readonly reason?: 'no_approved_seed';\n178\t readonly seedWalkCount: number;\n179\t readonly expandedNodeCount: number;\n180\t /**\n181\t * 시드 walk 중 하나라도 resultCap 초과로 절단됐는가 (S5-5 CO6-2).\n182\t * silent 절단된 부분집합에서 정답률을 재면 baseline 이 왜곡되므로 측정\n183\t * 무결성 상 반드시 surface — true 면 해당 baseline 표본은 절단 보정 필요.\n184\t */\n185\t readonly truncated: boolean;\n186\t readonly maxDepth: number;\n187\t readonly resultCap: number;\n188\t readonly edgeTypeWhitelist: ReadonlyArray<string>;\n189\t /**\n190\t * debug=true 한정 — graph 확장 전체집합 (dedup 후, baseline 교집합 제외분.\n191\t * 교집합 노드는 `baseline.results` 로 이미 관측 가능). 진단 규약(MASTER_PLAN\n192\t * WS-4 4c / G-WS4 ③): 정답 노드가 이 집합에 있으나 `results` top-K 밖 =\n193\t * **랭크미달**, 이 집합에도 `baseline.results` 에도 없음 = **미도달**.\n194\t * 기본 off — 응답 계약 additive (기존 소비자 shape 불변).\n195\t */\n196\t readonly expandedNodes?: ReadonlyArray<GraphExpansionDebugNode>;\n197\t}\n198\t\n199\t/** Phase 1-D lexical 모드 additive 메타 (plan rev3 §2-3 — 채점 코어 무접촉 필드). */\n200\tinterface LexicalFusionMeta {\n201\t readonly eps: number;\n202\t readonly poolSize: number;\n203\t /\*\* pool 중 lexical 신호(lexScore>0)가 붙은 후보 수. */\n204\t readonly lexMatchedCount: number;\n205\t /** 재정렬로 top-K 에서 밀려난 원 baseline hit 수 (M-2(iii) — regression 귀속용). \*/\n206\t readonly displacedBaselineHits: number;\n207\t readonly matchedTokens: ReadonlyArray<string>;\n208\t /** debug 한정 — pool 밖 lexical 히트 id (랭킹 무개입, 관측 전용 — rev3 Anchor 2). */\n209\t readonly lexicalOnlyObserved?: ReadonlyArray<string>;\n210\t}\n211\t\n212\tinterface GraphSearchResponse {\n213\t readonly query: string;\n214\t readonly examId: ExamId;\n215\t readonly topK: number;\n216\t /\*\* vector-only Stage1+2+3 결과 (S5-6 A/B 비교 baseline — 그대로 보존). */\n217\t readonly baseline: UserSearchResult;\n218\t readonly graphExpansion: GraphExpansionMeta;\n219\t /** baseline + graph 확장 병합 후 Stage 3 단일 진실원 재정렬 top-K. \*/\n220\t readonly results: ReadonlyArray<UserSearchHit>;\n221\t /** mode='lexical' 한정 additive (graph 모드 응답 byte-불변 — G-1D-2). */\n222\t readonly lexicalFusion?: LexicalFusionMeta;\n223\t}\n224\t\n225\t/\*\*\n226\t * Phase 1-D 재정렬 — ε-양자화 밴드키 **전순서** (plan rev3 §2-2, 리뷰 C-1 수리).\n227\t *\n228\t * 정렬키 = (truthWeight DESC, band DESC, lexScore DESC, score DESC),\n229\t * band = eps>0 ? floor(score/eps) : score — **전 키가 원소 단독 함수** = 추이성\n230\t * 구성적 보장(쌍별 ε-band 비교자는 비추이 → sort 미정의 동작 실증·폐기).\n231\t * 성질: lex 전원 0 → compareByTruthWeightThenScore 완전 동치(동점 포함) /\n232\t * ε=0 → 정확 동점에서만 lex 개입(순수 tiebreak 의도 동작). 경계쌍 비대칭\n233\t * (0.599/0.601 상이 band) = 양자화의 문서화된 대가.\n234\t * tie 최후 = 안정 정렬 입력순(ES2019 보장 — 원 비교자와 동일 관례).\n235\t */\n236\texport function lexicalRerank(\n237\t pool: ReadonlyArray<UserSearchHit>,\n238\t lexScores: ReadonlyMap<string, number>,\n239\t eps: number,\n240\t): ReadonlyArray<UserSearchHit> {\n241\t const keyed = pool.map((hit) => ({\n242\t hit,\n243\t lex: lexScores.get(hit.id) ?? 0,\n244\t band: eps > 0 ? Math.floor(hit.score / eps) : hit.score,\n245\t }));\n246\t const sorted = [...keyed].sort(\n247\t (a, b) =>\n248\t b.hit.truthWeight - a.hit.truthWeight ||\n249\t b.band - a.band ||\n250\t b.lex - a.lex ||\n251\t b.hit.score - a.hit.score,\n252\t );\n253\t return sorted.map((k) => k.hit);\n254\t}\n255\t\n256\texport function createGraphSearchRoutes(): Hono<{ Bindings: GraphSearchRouteBindings }> {\n257\t const app = new Hono<{ Bindings: GraphSearchRouteBindings }>();\n258\t\n259\t app.post('/', async (c) => {\n260\t const limiter = c.env.SEARCH_RATE_LIMITER_IP;\n261\t if (limiter !== undefined) {\n262\t const ip = getClientIp(c);\n263\t const { success } = await limiter.limit({ key: ip });\n264\t if (!success) {\n265\t c.header('Retry-After', '60');\n266\t return c.json({ error: 'TOO_MANY_REQUESTS' }, 429);\n267\t }\n268\t }\n269\t\n270\t const raw = await c.req.json().catch(() => null);\n271\t const parsed = GraphSearchBodySchema.safeParse(raw);\n272\t if (!parsed.success) {\n273\t return c.json({ error: ErrorCode.VALIDATION_ERROR, details: parsed.error.format() }, 400);\n274\t }\n275\t const { examId: rawExamId, query, topK, maxDepth, resultCap, debug, mode, eps } = parsed.data;\n276\t\n277\t let examId: ExamId;\n278\t try {\n279\t examId = assertValidExamId(rawExamId);\n280\t } catch {\n281\t return c.json({ error: ErrorCode.VALIDATION_ERROR, message: 'INVALID_EXAM_ID' }, 400);\n282\t }\n283\t\n284\t const env = c.env.ENVIRONMENT ?? 'production';\n285\t const logger = createLogger({\n286\t service: 'thepick-api',\n287\t environment: env as LoggerEnvironment,\n288\t });\n289\t // S5-5 CO6-3: 성공 경로도 elapsed 측정 — silent degradation(상태 회귀로\n290\t // expansion 0, 절단) 을 on-call/측정에서 관측 가능하게. 측정 무결성 직결.\n291\t const startedAt = Date.now();\n292\t const logOk = (meta: GraphExpansionMeta): void => {\n293\t logger.info('graph_search_ok', {\n294\t module: 'search/graph',\n295\t examId,\n296\t applied: meta.applied,\n297\t reason: meta.reason,\n298\t seedWalkCount: meta.seedWalkCount,\n299\t expandedNodeCount: meta.expandedNodeCount,\n300\t truncated: meta.truncated,\n301\t // 측정 경로 식별 — debug=true 는 query 상한 우회·payload 확대 요청이라\n302\t // on-call 이 정상/측정 트래픽을 로그에서 분리 가능해야 한다 (4c).\n303\t debug,\n304\t elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n305\t });\n306\t };\n307\t\n308\t try {\n309\t const deps: UserSearchDeps = {\n310\t ai: c.env.AI,\n311\t vectorize: c.env.VECTORIZE,\n312\t db: c.env.DB as unknown as UserSearchD1,\n313\t };\n314\t\n315\t // 1. vector-only baseline (기존 함수 재사용 — 정상 경로 불변).\n316\t // 임베딩 1회 계산 → baseline 에 재사용 (Workers AI 호출 절감).\n317\t const queryEmbedding = await embedQuery(c.env.AI, query);\n318\t const baseline = await searchKnowledgeNodesForUser(deps, examId, query, topK, {\n319\t precomputedEmbedding: queryEmbedding,\n320\t });\n321\t\n322\t const walkDb = c.env.DB as unknown as GraphWalkD1;\n323\t const baselineIds = new Set(baseline.results.map((h) => h.id));\n324\t\n325\t // 시드 = baseline approved 결과(이미 truth_weight 재정렬됨) 상위 N.\n326\t const seeds = baseline.results.slice(0, GRAPH_SEED_WALK_LIMIT);\n327\t\n328\t // 시드 0건 = graceful/stage2=0. graph-walk 는 approved 시드 필요 →\n329\t // 확장 미적용 명시 (silent fallback 금지, fail-loud 와 구분되는 정상).\n330\t if (seeds.length === 0) {\n331\t const meta: GraphExpansionMeta = {\n332\t applied: false,\n333\t reason: 'no_approved_seed',\n334\t seedWalkCount: 0,\n335\t expandedNodeCount: 0,\n336\t truncated: false,\n337\t maxDepth: maxDepth ?? 0,\n338\t resultCap: resultCap ?? 0,\n339\t edgeTypeWhitelist: [],\n340\t // debug 계약 일관성 — 플래그 on 이면 필드 상시 존재 (walk 미실행 = 빈 집합)\n341\t ...(debug ? { expandedNodes: [] as ReadonlyArray<GraphExpansionDebugNode> } : {}),\n342\t };\n343\t const resp: GraphSearchResponse = {\n344\t query,\n345\t examId,\n346\t topK: baseline.topK,\n347\t baseline,\n348\t graphExpansion: meta,\n349\t results: baseline.results,\n350\t };\n351\t logOk(meta);\n352\t return c.json(resp);\n353\t }\n354\t\n355\t // ── Phase 1-D lexical 모드 (plan rev3 §2 — graph-walk 무접촉·주입 없음) ──\n356\t // applied 술어 = graph 의 no_approved_seed 와 동일(위 분기 공유) → 채점\n357\t // 코어 제외집합이 3열 구성적 동일(M-1). 재정렬 대상 = vector pool 만(M-2).\n358\t if (mode === 'lexical') {\n359\t const pool = await searchKnowledgeNodesForUser(deps, examId, query, MAX_RESULT_TOP_K, {\n360\t precomputedEmbedding: queryEmbedding,\n361\t });\n362\t const lex = await runKeywordFallback(c.env.DB as unknown as UserSearchD1, examId, query);\n363\t const lexScores = new Map(lex.results.map((h) => [h.id, h.score]));\n364\t const reranked = lexicalRerank(pool.results, lexScores, eps);\n365\t const results = reranked.slice(0, topK);\n366\t\n367\t const resultIds = new Set(results.map((h) => h.id));\n368\t const poolIds = new Set(pool.results.map((h) => h.id));\n369\t const lexFusion: LexicalFusionMeta = {\n370\t eps,\n371\t poolSize: pool.results.length,\n372\t lexMatchedCount: pool.results.filter((h) => (lexScores.get(h.id) ?? 0) > 0).length,\n373\t displacedBaselineHits: baseline.results.filter((h) => !resultIds.has(h.id)).length,\n374\t matchedTokens: lex.matchedTokens,\n375\t ...(debug\n376\t ? {\n377\t lexicalOnlyObserved: lex.results.filter((h) => !poolIds.has(h.id)).map((h) => h.id),\n378\t }\n379\t : {}),\n380\t };\n381\t // 채점 코어 계약 유지(M-1): graphExpansion {applied,truncated} 필수 소비 —\n382\t // no-seed 분기 관례값(0/[]/false) 준용(rev3 m-N3). walk 미실행 = expandedNodes 항상 빈 집합.\n383\t const meta: GraphExpansionMeta = {\n384\t applied: true,\n385\t seedWalkCount: 0,\n386\t expandedNodeCount: 0,\n387\t truncated: false,\n388\t maxDepth: 0,\n389\t resultCap: 0,\n390\t edgeTypeWhitelist: [],\n391\t ...(debug ? { expandedNodes: [] as ReadonlyArray<GraphExpansionDebugNode> } : {}),\n392\t };\n393\t // lexical 전용 로그 라인 (G-1D-6 — logOk 는 GraphExpansionMeta 형상 전용이라 별도 어댑트).\n394\t logger.info('lexical_search_ok', {\n395\t module: 'search/graph',\n396\t examId,\n397\t mode,\n398\t eps,\n399\t poolSize: lexFusion.poolSize,\n400\t lexMatchedCount: lexFusion.lexMatchedCount,\n401\t displacedBaselineHits: lexFusion.displacedBaselineHits,\n402\t debug,\n403\t elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n404\t });\n405\t const resp: GraphSearchResponse = {\n406\t query,\n407\t examId,\n408\t topK: baseline.topK,\n409\t baseline,\n410\t graphExpansion: meta,\n411\t results,\n412\t lexicalFusion: lexFusion,\n413\t };\n414\t return c.json(resp);\n415\t }\n416\t\n417\t // 2. 각 시드 N-hop 확장. graph-walk 실패는 전파 (빈 결과로 삼키지 않음).\n418\t //\n419\t // ★ mid-loop 실패 계약 (S5-5 CO6-4(c) — 명시 pin): 5-seed 중 임의\n420\t // 시드의 graphWalk 가 throw 하면 부분 확장으로 degrade 하지 않고\n421\t // **fail-loud** (전파 → catch → 5xx). graph-walk 모듈 교리(\"빈\n422\t // 결과로 삼키지 않는다\", index.ts:252)와 정합 — 부분 확장 집합에서\n423\t // 정답률을 재면 baseline 측정이 silent 왜곡되므로, 측정 무결성 상\n424\t // 부분 성공 < 전체 실패. (S5-7 A 통합 시 학습자 UX 차원에서\n425\t // per-seed graceful 재검토 가능 — 현 옵션 C 측정 경로는 fail-loud.)\n426\t //\n427\t // 확장 노드는 GraphWalkNode 그대로 dedup(Map) — S5-5 CO6-1: graph-walk\n428\t // projection 이 description 동봉 → 잉여 2차 fetchApprovedNodes 제거.\n429\t const expandedNodes = new Map<string, GraphWalkNode>();\n430\t let effectiveMaxDepth = 0;\n431\t let effectiveResultCap = 0;\n432\t let edgeTypeWhitelist: ReadonlyArray<string> = [];\n433\t let anyTruncated = false;\n434\t for (const seed of seeds) {\n435\t const walk = await graphWalk(examId, walkDb, seed.id, {\n436\t ...(maxDepth !== undefined ? 구성적 보장(쌍별 ε-band 비교자는 비추이 → sort 미정의 동작 실증·폐기).\n * 성질: lex 전원 0 → compareByTruthWeightThenScore 완전 동치(동점 포함) /\n * ε=0 → 정확 동점에서만 lex 개입(순수 tiebreak 의도 동작). 경계쌍 비대칭\n * (0.599/0.601 상이 band) = 양자화의 문서화된 대가.\n * tie 최후 = 안정 정렬 입력순(ES2019 보장 — 원 비교자와 동일 관례).\n */\nexport function lexicalRerank(\n pool: ReadonlyArray<UserSearchHit>,\n lexScores: ReadonlyMap<string, number>,\n eps: number,\n): ReadonlyArray<UserSearchHit> {\n const keyed = pool.map((hit) => ({\n hit,\n lex: lexScores.get(hit.id) ?? 0,\n band: eps > 0 ? Math.floor(hit.score / eps) : hit.score,\n }));\n const sorted = [...keyed].sort(\n (a, b) =>\n b.hit.truthWeight - a.hit.truthWeight ||\n b.band - a.band ||\n b.lex - a.lex ||\n b.hit.score - a.hit.score,\n );\n return sorted.map((k) => k.hit);\n}\n\nexport function createGraphSearchRoutes(): Hono<{ Bindings: GraphSearchRouteBindings }> {\n const app = new Hono<{ Bindings: GraphSearchRouteBindings }>();\n\n app.post('/', async (c) => {\n const limiter = c.env.SEARCH_RATE_LIMITER_IP;\n if (limiter !== undefined) {\n const ip = getClientIp(c);\n const { success } = await limiter.limit({ key: ip });\n if (!success) {\n c.header('Retry-After', '60');\n return c.json({ error: 'TOO_MANY_REQUESTS' }, 429);\n }\n }\n\n const raw = await c.req.json().catch(() => null);\n const parsed = GraphSearchBodySchema.safeParse(raw);\n if (!parsed.success) {\n return c.json({ error: ErrorCode.VALIDATION_ERROR, details: parsed.error.format() }, 400);\n }\n const { examId: rawExamId, query, topK, maxDepth, resultCap, debug, mode, eps } = parsed.data;\n\n let examId: ExamId;\n try {\n examId = assertValidExamId(rawExamId);\n } catch {\n return c.json({ error: ErrorCode.VALIDATION_ERROR, message: 'INVALID_EXAM_ID' }, 400);\n }\n\n const env = c.env.ENVIRONMENT ?? 'production';\n const logger = createLogger({\n service: 'thepick-api',\n environment: env as LoggerEnvironment,\n });\n // S5-5 CO6-3: 성공 경로도 elapsed 측정 — silent degradation(상태 회귀로\n // expansion 0, 절단) 을 on-call/측정에서 관측 가능하게. 측정 무결성 직결.\n const startedAt = Date.now();\n const logOk = (meta: GraphExpansionMeta): void => {\n logger.info('graph_search_ok', {\n module: 'search/graph',\n examId,\n applied: meta.applied,\n reason: meta.reason,\n seedWalkCount: meta.seedWalkCount,\n expandedNodeCount: meta.expandedNodeCount,\n truncated: meta.truncated,\n // 측정 경로 식별 — debug=true 는 query 상한 우회·payload 확대 요청이라\n // on-call 이 정상/측정 트래픽을 로그에서 분리 가능해야 한다 (4c).\n debug,\n elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n });\n };\n\n try {\n const deps: UserSearchDeps = {\n ai: c.env.AI,\n vectorize: c.env.VECTORIZE,\n db: c.env.DB as unknown as UserSearchD1,\n };\n\n // 1. vector-only baseline (기존 함수 재사용 — 정상 경로 불변).\n // 임베딩 1회 계산 → baseline 에 재사용 (Workers AI 호출 절감).\n const queryEmbedding = await embedQuery(c.env.AI, query);\n const baseline = await searchKnowledgeNodesForUser(deps, examId, query, topK, {\n precomputedEmbedding: queryEmbedding,\n });\n\n const walkDb = c.env.DB as unknown as GraphWalkD1;\n const baselineIds = new Set(baseline.results.map((h) => h.id));\n\n // 시드 = baseline approved 결과(이미 truth_weight 재정렬됨) 상위 N.\n const seeds = baseline.results.slice(0, GRAPH_SEED_WALK_LIMIT);\n\n // 시드 0건 = graceful/stage2=0. graph-walk 는 approved 시드 필요 →\n // 확장 미적용 명시 (silent fallback 금지, fail-loud 와 구분되는 정상).\n if (seeds.length === 0) {\n const meta: GraphExpansionMeta = {\n applied: false,\n reason: 'no_approved_seed',\n seedWalkCount: 0,\n expandedNodeCount: 0,\n truncated: false,\n maxDepth: maxDepth ?? 0,\n resultCap: resultCap ?? 0,\n edgeTypeWhitelist: [],\n // debug 계약 일관성 — 플래그 on 이면 필드 상시 존재 (walk 미실행 = 빈 집합)\n ...(debug ? { expandedNodes: [] as ReadonlyArray<GraphExpansionDebugNode> } : {}),\n };\n const resp: GraphSearchResponse = {\n query,\n examId,\n topK: baseline.topK,\n baseline,\n graphExpansion: meta,\n results: baseline.results,\n };\n logOk(meta);\n return c.json(resp);\n }\n\n // ── Phase 1-D lexical 모드 (plan rev3 §2 — graph-walk 무접촉·주입 없음) ──\n // applied 술어 = graph 의 no_approved_seed 와 동일(위 분기 공유) → 채점\n // 코어 제외집합이 3열 구성적 동일(M-1). 재정렬 대상 = vector pool 만(M-2).\n if (mode === 'lexical') {\n const pool = await searchKnowledgeNodesForUser(deps, examId, query, MAX_RESULT_TOP_K, {\n precomputedEmbedding: queryEmbedding,\n });\n const lex = await runKeywordFallback(c.env.DB as unknown as UserSearchD1, examId, query);\n const lexScores = new Map(lex.results.map((h) => [h.id, h.score]));\n const reranked = lexicalRerank(pool.results, lexScores, eps);\n const results = reranked.slice(0, topK);\n\n const resultIds = new Set(results.map((h) => h.id));\n const poolIds = new Set(pool.results.map((h) => h.id));\n const lexFusion: LexicalFusionMeta = {\n eps,\n poolSize: pool.results.length,\n lexMatchedCount: pool.results.filter((h) => (lexScores.get(h.id) ?? 0) > 0).length,\n displacedBaselineHits: baseline.results.filter((h) => !resultIds.has(h.id)).length,\n matchedTokens: lex.matchedTokens,\n ...(debug\n ? {\n lexicalOnlyObserved: lex.results.filter((h) => !poolIds.has(h.id)).map((h) => h.id),\n }\n : {}),\n };\n // 채점 코어 계약 유지(M-1): graphExpansion {applied,truncated} 필수 소비 —\n // no-seed 분기 관례값(0/[]/false) 준용(rev3 m-N3). walk 미실행 = expandedNodes 항상 빈 집합.\n const meta: GraphExpansionMeta = {\n applied: true,\n seedWalkCount: 0,\n expandedNodeCount: 0,\n truncated: false,\n maxDepth: 0,\n resultCap: 0,\n edgeTypeWhitelist: [],\n ...(debug ? { expandedNodes: [] as ReadonlyArray<GraphExpansionDebugNode> } : {}),\n };\n // lexical 전용 로그 라인 (G-1D-6 — logOk 는 GraphExpansionMeta 형상 전용이라 별도 어댑트).\n logger.info('lexical_search_ok', {\n module: 'search/graph',\n examId,\n mode,\n eps,\n poolSize: lexFusion.poolSize,\n lexMatchedCount: lexFusion.lexMatchedCount,\n displacedBaselineHits: lexFusion.displacedBaselineHits,\n debug,\n elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n });\n const resp: GraphSearchResponse = {\n query,\n examId,\n topK: baseline.topK,\n baseline,\n graphExpansion: meta,\n results,\n lexicalFusion: lexFusion,\n };\n return c.json(resp);\n }\n\n // 2. 각 시드 N-hop 확장. graph-walk 실패는 전파 (빈 결과로 삼키지 않음).\n //\n // ★ mid-loop 실패 계약 (S5-5 CO6-4(c) — 명시 pin): 5-seed 중 임의\n // 시드의 graphWalk 가 throw 하면 부분 확장으로 degrade 하지 않고\n // **fail-loud** (전파 → catch → 5xx). graph-walk 모듈 교리(\"빈\n // 결과로 삼키지 않는다\", index.ts:252)와 정합 — 부분 확장 집합에서\n // 정답률을 재면 baseline 측정이 silent 왜곡되므로, 측정 무결성 상\n // 부분 성공 < 전체 실패. (S5-7 A 통합 시 학습자 UX 차원에서\n // per-seed graceful 재검토 가능 — 현 옵션 C 측정 경로는 fail-loud.)\n //\n // 확장 노드는 GraphWalkNode 그대로 dedup(Map) — S5-5 CO6-1: graph-walk\n // projection 이 description 동봉 → 잉여 2차 fetchApprovedNodes 제거.\n const expandedNodes = new Map<string, GraphWalkNode>();\n let effectiveMaxDepth = 0;\n let effectiveResultCap = 0;\n let edgeTypeWhitelist: ReadonlyArray<string> = [];\n let anyTruncated = false;\n for (const seed of seeds) {\n const walk = await graphWalk(examId, walkDb, seed.id, {\n ...(maxDepth !== undefined ? { maxDepth } : {}),\n ...(resultCap !== undefined ? { resultCap } : {}),\n });\n // 엔진이 clamp 한 실효값 노출 (D-2 MAX_ALLOWED_DEPTH=4 등 — 호출자 검사 가능)\n effectiveMaxDepth = walk.maxDepth;\n effectiveResultCap = walk.resultCap;\n edgeTypeWhitelist = walk.edgeTypeWhitelist;\n // S5-5 CO6-2: 어느 시드든 절단되면 표면화 (silent 절단 차단).\n if (walk.truncated) anyTruncated = true;\n for (const n of walk.nodes) {\n // baseline 교집합 노드는 baseline hit(실 vector score)이 우선 →\n // 확장에서 제외 (CO6-4(a) — 충돌 시 baseline 보존). 시드 간 중복은\n // Map 키로 1회 collapse.\n if (!baselineIds.has(n.id) && !expandedNodes.has(n.id)) {\n expandedNodes.set(n.id, n);\n }\n }\n }\n\n // 3. 확장 노드 → hit. buildHit 단일 진실원 공유(CO-3) — graph-walk 노드를\n // NodeHitSource 로 매핑(2차 DB 조회 없음, CO6-1). 확장 노드 vector\n // score=0 (graph 관계로 편입 — truth_weight 가 정렬 지배, score 는\n // 동 weight tiebreak 만).\n const expandedHits = [...expandedNodes.values()].map((n) => {\n const src: NodeHitSource = {\n id: n.id,\n type: n.type,\n name: n.name,\n description: n.description,\n page_ref: n.pageRef,\n truth_weight: n.truthWeight,\n };\n return buildHit(src, 0);\n });\n\n // 4. 병합 + Stage 3 단일 진실원 재정렬 (CO-3). baseline hit 은 실 vector\n // score 보존(교집합은 위에서 baseline 우선 처리 → 중복 없음).\n const merged = [...baseline.results, ...expandedHits].sort(compareByTruthWeightThenScore);\n\n const meta: GraphExpansionMeta = {\n applied: true,\n seedWalkCount: seeds.length,\n expandedNodeCount: expandedNodes.size,\n truncated: anyTruncated,\n maxDepth: effectiveMaxDepth,\n resultCap: effectiveResultCap,\n edgeTypeWhitelist,\n // WS-4 4c: debug=true 한정 확장 전체집합 surface — 랭크미달 vs 미도달\n // 진단 (description 제외 = GraphExpansionDebugNode 주석 참조).\n ...(debug\n ? {\n expandedNodes: [...expandedNodes.values()].map(\n (n): GraphExpansionDebugNode => ({\n id: n.id,\n depth: n.depth,\n type: n.type,\n name: n.name,\n truthWeight: n.truthWeight,\n pageRef: n.pageRef,\n }),\n ),\n }\n : {}),\n };\n const resp: GraphSearchResponse = {\n query,\n examId,\n topK: baseline.topK,\n baseline,\n graphExpansion: meta,\n results: merged.slice(0, baseline.topK),\n };\n logOk(meta);\n return c.json(resp);\n } catch (err) {\n // fail-loud — graph-walk / user-search 실패 전파 (빈 결과 은폐 금지).\n const isDev = env === 'dev' || env === 'development' || env === 'test';\n const cause = (err as { cause?: unknown }).cause;\n const causeName = cause instanceof Error ? cause.name : undefined;\n\n if (err instanceof GraphWalkError) {\n logger.error('graph_search_walk_failed', undefined, {\n module: 'search/graph',\n phase: err.phase,\n examId,\n causeName,\n elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n });\n return c.json(\n {\n error: ErrorCode.AI_GENERATION_ERROR,\n phase: err.phase,\n message: isDev ? docs/plans/phase2a-multi-path-fallback.plan.md §3.1\n20\t * - docs/architecture/SEARCH_PIPELINE.md §5\n21\t * - docs/adr/ADR-015-multi-path-fallback-pipeline.md\n22\t */\n23\t\n24\timport { TRUTH_WEIGHTS, type ExamId, type NodeType } from '@thepick/shared';\n25\timport { parsePageRefToInt } from '../../vectorize/page-ref.js';\n26\timport { buildApprovedNodesQuery } from '../approved-nodes-sql.js';\n27\timport { UserSearchError, type UserSearchD1, type UserSearchHit } from '../user-search.js';\n28\t\n29\t/** Stage 2 keyword 매칭 최소 토큰 길이 (1자 noise 차단). \*/\n30\texport const KEYWORD_MIN_TOKEN_LEN = 2;\n31\t\n32\t/** Stage 2 keyword 매칭 응답 max top-K. */\n33\texport const KEYWORD_FALLBACK_MAX_TOP_K = 10;\n34\t\n35\t/\*\* 토큰별 LIKE 매칭 최대 후보 (D1 부담 제한). */\n36\texport const KEYWORD_PER_TOKEN_LIMIT = 50;\n37\t\n38\tinterface KeywordMatchRow {\n39\t readonly id: string;\n40\t readonly type: string;\n41\t readonly name: string;\n42\t readonly description: string | null;\n43\t readonly page_ref: string | null;\n44\t readonly truth_weight: number;\n45\t readonly is_current_active: number;\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\texport interface KeywordFallbackResult {\n49\t readonly source: 'keyword-fallback';\n50\t readonly matchedTokens: ReadonlyArray<string>;\n51\t readonly results: ReadonlyArray<UserSearchHit>;\n52\t}\n53\t\n54\t/**\n55\t * 자주 등장하는 한국어 조사/어미 — 토큰 끝에서 제거 (정확도 보강).\n56\t *\n57\t * Pass 3 M3 + Pass 1 MAJ-1 흡수 (Session 059): \"낙엽률이\" → \"낙엽률\".\n58\t * 형태소 분석 미적용 단순 패턴 매칭이라 100% 커버 X — 정확도 보강 (D-MPF-1=A 정합).\n59\t \*/\n60\tconst KOREAN_PARTICLE_PATTERN =\n61\t /(이|가|을|를|의|에|에서|에게|로|으로|에서는|에는|와|과|도|만|는|은|이라|이라고|이라는|입니다|입니까|이며|이고|이지만|인지|이지|이군요|어요|어서|에서도|까지|부터|마다)$/;\n62\t\n63\t/**\n64\t * query 를 공백 + 한국어 조사 기반 단순 토큰화.\n65\t *\n66\t * Pass 1 MAJ-1 흡수 (Session 059): 한국어 특수문자 (가운뎃점 · / 인용부호 “” /\n67\t * 물결 〜 / 일본식 점 、 / 모지 「」『』) 포함 — Unicode property `\\p{P}` `\\p{S}`.\n68\t * Pass 3 M3 흡수: 토큰 끝 한국어 조사/어미 제거 (\"낙엽률이\" → \"낙엽률\").\n69\t *\n70\t * 한계 (D-MPF-1=A 정합): 형태소 분석 없음 → 100% 커버 X. SP-T06 < 85% 미달 시\n71\t * 옵션 B (bge-m3 reranking) 보강 의무.\n72\t */\n73\texport function tokenizeQuery(query: string): ReadonlyArray<string> {\n74\t return query\n75\t .trim()\n76\t .split(/\\s+/)\n77\t .map((t) => t.replace(/[\\p{P}\\p{S}]/gu, ''))\n78\t .map((t) => {\n79\t // 조사 제거 — KEYWORD_MIN_TOKEN_LEN 이상 보존 시에만 (단, 짧은 어미 1자 제거는 허용)\n80\t const stripped = t.replace(KOREAN_PARTICLE_PATTERN, '');\n81\t return stripped.length >= KEYWORD_MIN_TOKEN_LEN ? stripped : t;\n82\t })\n83\t .filter((t) => t.length >= KEYWORD_MIN_TOKEN_LEN);\n84\t}\n85\t\n86\t/\*\*\n87\t * Stage 2 Keyword Fallback — D1 LIKE 매칭 + Stage 2 hard filter 정합.\n88\t *\n89\t * @returns matchedTokens (실제 매칭된 토큰 목록) + results (top-K hits)\n90\t * 매칭 0건 시 results=[] (Stage 3 진입 신호)\n91\t */\n92\texport async function runKeywordFallback(\n93\t db: UserSearchD1,\n94\t examId: ExamId,\n95\t query: string,\n96\t topK: number = KEYWORD_FALLBACK_MAX_TOP_K,\n97\t): Promise<KeywordFallbackResult> {\n98\t if (!examId || (examId as string).trim() === '') {\n99\t throw new UserSearchError('examId is required (Hard Rule 16 시험 경계 강제)', 'filter');\n100\t }\n101\t if (!query || query.trim() === '') {\n102\t throw new UserSearchError('query must be non-empty', 'filter');\n103\t }\n104\t\n105\t const tokens = tokenizeQuery(query);\n106\t if (tokens.length === 0) {\n107\t return { source: 'keyword-fallback', matchedTokens: [], results: [] };\n108\t }\n109\t\n110\t // 토큰별 후보 수집 — Pass 1 MAJ-2 + CRIT-2 흡수 (Session 059):\n111\t // - Promise.all 병렬화: N 토큰 × ~50ms D1 round-trip → 1 round-trip 동시화 (latency)\n112\t // - per-token try/catch 격리: 1 토큰 throw 가 전체 fallback 을 깨뜨리지 않음 (graceful)\n113\t // - 모든 토큰 throw 시 (totalFailed === tokens.length) 명시적 throw (조용한 0건 금지)\n114\t const tokenResults = await Promise.all(\n115\t tokens.map(async (token) => {\n116\t try {\n117\t const rows = await fetchTokenMatches(db, token);\n118\t return { token, rows, error: null as unknown };\n119\t } catch (err) {\n120\t return { token, rows: [] as ReadonlyArray<KeywordMatchRow>, error: err };\n121\t }\n122\t }),\n123\t );\n124\t\n125\t const totalFailed = tokenResults.filter((r) => r.error !== null).length;\n126\t if (totalFailed === tokens.length) {\n127\t // 모든 토큰 실패 — 첫 번째 cause 보존 + Pass 3 M2 정합 sanitized message\n128\t const firstError = tokenResults.find((r) => r.error !== null)?.error;\n129\t throw new UserSearchError('Stage 2 keyword fallback 전체 실패', 'filter', firstError);\n130\t }\n131\t\n132\t const matchByNode = new Map<string, { row: KeywordMatchRow; matched: Set<string> }>();\n133\t const actualMatched = new Set<string>();\n134\t\n135\t for (const { token, rows } of tokenResults) {\n136\t if (rows.length > 0) actualMatched.add(token);\n137\t for (const row of rows) {\n138\t const existing = matchByNode.get(row.id);\n139\t if (existing !== undefined) {\n140\t existing.matched.add(token);\n141\t } else {\n142\t matchByNode.set(row.id, { row, matched: new Set([token]) });\n143\t }\n144\t }\n145\t }\n146\t\n147\t if (matchByNode.size === 0) {\n148\t return { source: 'keyword-fallback', matchedTokens: [], results: [] };\n149\t }\n150\t\n151\t // 일치 토큰 수 DESC → truth_weight DESC → name 사전식 ASC\n152\t const ranked = Array.from(matchByNode.values()).sort((a, b) => {\n153\t if (a.matched.size !== b.matched.size) return b.matched.size - a.matched.size;\n154\t const wA = TRUTH_WEIGHTS[a.row.type as NodeType] ?? a.row.truth_weight;\n155\t const wB = TRUTH_WEIGHTS[b.row.type as NodeType] ?? b.row.truth_weight;\n156\t if (wA !== wB) return wB - wA;\n157\t return a.row.name.localeCompare(b.row.name);\n158\t });\n159\t\n160\t const effectiveTopK = Math.min(Math.max(topK, 1), KEYWORD_FALLBACK_MAX_TOP_K);\n161\t const results: UserSearchHit[] = ranked.slice(0, effectiveTopK).map(({ row, matched }) => {\n162\t const truthWeight = TRUTH_WEIGHTS[row.type as NodeType] ?? row.truth_weight;\n163\t return {\n164\t id: row.id,\n165\t // keyword score: 일치 토큰 비율 (0.0~1.0). vector similarity 와 의미 다르나 동일 응답 shape.\n166\t score: matched.size / tokens.length,\n167\t type: row.type,\n168\t truthWeight,\n169\t pageRef: parsePageRefToInt(row.page_ref).value,\n170\t name: row.name,\n171\t description: row.description,\n172\t };\n173\t });\n174\t\n175\t return {\n176\t source: 'keyword-fallback',\n177\t matchedTokens: Array.from(actualMatched),\n178\t results,\n179\t };\n180\t}\n181\t\n182\t/**\n183\t * 단일 토큰 LIKE 매칭 — Stage 2 정합 (status='approved' + is_current_active=1).\n184\t *\n185\t * SQL 정책 (Pass 3 M2 흡수, Session 058):\n186\t * - status_transitions LEFT JOIN ROW_NUMBER OVER PARTITION (최신 to_status)\n187\t * - knowledge_nodes.status 컬럼은 INSERT snapshot — 실제 status 는 status_transitions\n188\t */\n189\tasync function fetchTokenMatches(\n190\t db: UserSearchD1,\n191\t token: string,\n192\t): Promise<ReadonlyArray<KeywordMatchRow>> {\n193\t // SQL injection 방어 — '?' parameter binding 만 사용. token 본문 % 자체 escape 미필요\n194\t // (D1 SQLite 는 LIKE escape 별도 지정 시에만 활성). 정상 학습자 query 에 % 가 의미\n195\t // 있는 검색 의도가 부재해 단순 substring 매칭 안전.\n196\t const likePattern = `%${token}%`;\n197\t // status 도출 = approved-nodes-sql.ts 단일 진실원 공유 (CO-4 — 정상/graph/\n198\t // fallback 4 호출 측 동일 코어). LIKE 후보 한정은 status 코어 뒤 AND 결합\n199\t // (교환법칙 → 원 SQL 결과 집합 불변). status 코어엔 `?` 0개 → bind 순서\n200\t // (likePattern×2) 불변. LIMIT 은 코어 외부에서 부착.\n201\t const sql = `${buildApprovedNodesQuery({\n202\t    projection:\n203\t      'kn.id, kn.type, kn.name, kn.description, kn.page_ref, kn.truth_weight, kn.is_current_active',\n204\t    candidateFilter: '(kn.name LIKE ? OR kn.description LIKE ?)',\n205\t  })}\n206\t    LIMIT ${KEYWORD_PER_TOKEN_LIMIT}`;\n207\t try {\n208\t const result = await db.prepare(sql).bind(likePattern, likePattern).all<KeywordMatchRow>();\n209\t return result.results ?? [];\n210\t } catch (err) {\n211\t // Pass 3 M2 정합 — public message 에 SQL keyword 비포함. cause 보존.\n212\t throw new UserSearchError('Stage 2 keyword fallback 실패', 'filter', err);\n213\t }\n214\t}\n215\t"}]},"uuid":"6a513fbd-5153-439f-8419-6b5b2113becd","timestamp":"2026-07-07T05:38:33.083Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/soo/ClaudePro/ThePick/apps/api/src/search/multi-path-fallback/keyword-fallback.ts","content":"/**\n * Multi-Path Fallback Stage 2 — Keyword/N-gram Match (D1 LIKE/substring).\n *\n * D-MPF-1=A 채택 (Session 059): 형태소 분석기 (kkma/khaiii) 미사용. Workers 즉시\n * 가동을 위한 D1 LIKE 단순 매칭. 한국어 어미 변화 미대응 한계 → SP-T06 ≥ 85%\n * 미달 시 옵션 B (bge-m3 reranking) 보강 의무.\n *\n * 알고리즘:\n * 1. query 를 공백 기준 토큰화 (단순)\n * 2. 길이 ≥ 2 토큰만 추출 (1자 noise 차단)\n * 3. 각 토큰에 대해 D1 `name LIKE '%token%' OR description LIKE '%token%'` 매칭\n * 4. 매칭 노드의 일치 토큰 수 (matchScore) 로 정렬 + Stage 2 정합 (approved + active)\n * 5. top-K=10 반환\n *\n * Hard Rule 16/17 정합: examId 첫 인자 강제, EXAM_IDS 경유 100%.\n * Stage 2 정합 (ADR-013): is_current_active=1 + status='approved' (status_transitions JOIN).\n *\n * 근거:\n * - docs/plans/phase2a-multi-path-fallback.plan.md §3.1\n * - docs/architecture/SEARCH_PIPELINE.md §5\n * - docs/adr/ADR-015-multi-path-fallback-pipeline.md\n */\n\nimport { TRUTH_WEIGHTS, type ExamId, type NodeType } from '@thepick/shared';\nimport { parsePageRefToInt } from '../../vectorize/page-ref.js';\nimport { buildApprovedNodesQuery } from '../approved-nodes-sql.js';\nimport { UserSearchError, type UserSearchD1, type UserSearchHit } from '../user-search.js';\n\n/\*\* Stage 2 keyword 매칭 최소 토큰 길이 (1자 noise 차단). */\nexport const KEYWORD_MIN_TOKEN_LEN = 2;\n\n/** Stage 2 keyword 매칭 응답 max top-K. \*/\nexport const KEYWORD_FALLBACK_MAX_TOP_K = 10;\n\n/** 토큰별 LIKE 매칭 최대 후보 (D1 부담 제한). */\nexport const KEYWORD_PER_TOKEN_LIMIT = 50;\n\ninterface KeywordMatchRow {\n readonly id: string;\n readonly type: string;\n readonly name: string;\n readonly description: string | null;\n readonly page_ref: string | null;\n readonly truth_weight: number;\n readonly is_current_active: number;\n}\n\nexport interface KeywordFallbackResult {\n readonly source: 'keyword-fallback';\n readonly matchedTokens: ReadonlyArray<string>;\n readonly results: ReadonlyArray<UserSearchHit>;\n}\n\n/\*\*\n * 자주 등장하는 한국어 조사/어미 — 토큰 끝에서 제거 (정확도 보강).\n *\n * Pass 3 M3 + Pass 1 MAJ-1 흡수 (Session 059): \"낙엽률이\" → \"낙엽률\".\n * 형태소 분석 미적용 단순 패턴 매칭이라 100% 커버 X — 정확도 보강 (D-MPF-1=A 정합).\n */\nconst KOREAN_PARTICLE_PATTERN =\n /(이|가|을|를|의|에|에서|에게|로|으로|에서는|에는|와|과|도|만|는|은|이라|이라고|이라는|입니다|입니까|이며|이고|이지만|인지|이지|이군요|어요|어서|에서도|까지|부터|마다)$/;\n\n/**\n * query 를 공백 + 한국어 조사 기반 단순 토큰화.\n *\n * Pass 1 MAJ-1 흡수 (Session 059): 한국어 특수문자 (가운뎃점 · / 인용부호 “” /\n * 물결 〜 / 일본식 점 、 / 모지 「」『』) 포함 — Unicode property `\\p{P}` `\\p{S}`.\n * Pass 3 M3 흡수: 토큰 끝 한국어 조사/어미 제거 (\"낙엽률이\" → \"낙엽률\").\n *\n * 한계 (D-MPF-1=A 정합): 형태소 분석 없음 → 100% 커버 X. SP-T06 < 85% 미달 시\n * 옵션 B (bge-m3 reranking) 보강 의무.\n */\nexport function tokenizeQuery(query: string): ReadonlyArray<string> {\n  return query\n    .trim()\n    .split(/\\s+/)\n    .map((t) => t.replace(/[\\p{P}\\p{S}]/gu, ''))\n    .map((t) => {\n      // 조사 제거 — KEYWORD_MIN_TOKEN_LEN 이상 보존 시에만 (단, 짧은 어미 1자 제거는 허용)\n      const stripped = t.replace(KOREAN_PARTICLE_PATTERN, '');\n      return stripped.length >= KEYWORD_MIN_TOKEN_LEN ? stripped : t;\n    })\n    .filter((t) => t.length >= KEYWORD_MIN_TOKEN_LEN);\n}\n\n/**\n * Stage 2 Keyword Fallback — D1 LIKE 매칭 + Stage 2 hard filter 정합.\n *\n * @returns matchedTokens (실제 매칭된 토큰 목록) + results (top-K hits)\n *          매칭 0건 시 results=[] (Stage 3 진입 신호)\n */\nexport async function runKeywordFallback(\n  db: UserSearchD1,\n  examId: ExamId,\n  query: string,\n  topK: number = KEYWORD_FALLBACK_MAX_TOP_K,\n): Promise<KeywordFallbackResult> {\n  if (!examId || (examId as string).trim() === '') {\n    throw new UserSearchError('examId is required (Hard Rule 16 시험 경계 강제)', 'filter');\n  }\n  if (!query || query.trim() === '') {\n    throw new UserSearchError('query must be non-empty', 'filter');\n  }\n\n  const tokens = tokenizeQuery(query);\n  if (tokens.length === 0) {\n    return { source: 'keyword-fallback', matchedTokens: [], results: [] };\n  }\n\n  // 토큰별 후보 수집 — Pass 1 MAJ-2 + CRIT-2 흡수 (Session 059):\n  //   - Promise.all 병렬화: N 토큰 × ~50ms D1 round-trip → 1 round-trip 동시화 (latency)\n  //   - per-token try/catch 격리: 1 토큰 throw 가 전체 fallback 을 깨뜨리지 않음 (graceful)\n  //   - 모든 토큰 throw 시 (totalFailed === tokens.length) 명시적 throw (조용한 0건 금지)\n  const tokenResults = await Promise.all(\n    tokens.map(async (token) => {\n      try {\n        const rows = await fetchTokenMatches(db, token);\n        return { token, rows, error: null as unknown };\n      } catch (err) {\n        return { token, rows: [] as ReadonlyArray<KeywordMatchRow>, error: err };\n      }\n    }),\n  );\n\n  const totalFailed = tokenResults.filter((r) => r.error !== null).length;\n  if (totalFailed === tokens.length) {\n    // 모든 토큰 실패 — 첫 번째 cause 보존 + Pass 3 M2 정합 sanitized message\n    const firstError = tokenResults.find((r) => r.error !== null)?.error;\n    throw new UserSearchError('Stage 2 keyword fallback 전체 실패', 'filter', firstError);\n  }\n\n  const matchByNode = new Map<string, { row: KeywordMatchRow; matched: Set<string> }>();\n  const actualMatched = new Set<string>();\n\n  for (const { token, rows } of tokenResults) {\n    if (rows.length > 0) actualMatched.add(token);\n    for (const row of rows) {\n      const existing = matchByNode.get(row.id);\n      if (existing !== undefined) {\n        existing.matched.add(token);\n      } else {\n        matchByNode.set(row.id, { row, matched: new Set([token]) });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (matchByNode.size === 0) {\n    return { source: 'keyword-fallback', matchedTokens: [], results: [] };\n  }\n\n  // 일치 토큰 수 DESC → truth_weight DESC → name 사전식 ASC\n  const ranked = Array.from(matchByNode.values()).sort((a, b) => {\n    if (a.matched.size !== b.matched.size) return b.matched.size - a.matched.size;\n    const wA = TRUTH_WEIGHTS[a.row.type as NodeType] ?? a.row.truth_weight;\n    const wB = TRUTH_WEIGHTS[b.row.type as NodeType] ?? b.row.truth_weight;\n    if (wA !== wB) return wB - wA;\n    return a.row.name.localeCompare(b.row.name);\n  });\n\n  const effectiveTopK = Math.min(Math.max(topK, 1), KEYWORD_FALLBACK_MAX_TOP_K);\n  const results: UserSearchHit[] = ranked.slice(0, effectiveTopK).map(({ row, matched }) => {\n    const truthWeight = TRUTH_WEIGHTS[row.type as NodeType] ?? row.truth_weight;\n    return {\n      id: row.id,\n      // keyword score: 일치 토큰 비율 (0.0~1.0). vector similarity 와 의미 다르나 동일 응답 shape.\n      score: matched.size / tokens.length,\n      type: row.type,\n      truthWeight,\n      pageRef: parsePageRefToInt(row.page_ref).value,\n      name: row.name,\n      description: row.description,\n    };\n  });\n\n  return {\n    source: 'keyword-fallback',\n    matchedTokens: Array.from(actualMatched),\n    results,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * 단일 토큰 LIKE 매칭 — Stage 2 정합 (status='approved' + is_current_active=1).\n *\n * SQL 정책 (Pass 3 M2 흡수, Session 058):\n *   - status_transitions LEFT JOIN ROW_NUMBER OVER PARTITION (최신 to_status)\n *   - knowledge_nodes.status 컬럼은 INSERT snapshot — 실제 status 는 status_transitions\n */\nasync function fetchTokenMatches(\n  db: UserSearchD1,\n  token: string,\n): Promise<ReadonlyArray<KeywordMatchRow>> {\n  // SQL injection 방어 — '?' parameter binding 만 사용. token 본문 % 자체 escape 미필요\n  // (D1 SQLite 는 LIKE escape 별도 지정 시에만 활성). 정상 학습자 query 에 % 가 의미\n  // 있는 검색 의도가 부재해 단순 substring 매칭 안전.\n  const likePattern = `%${token}%`;\n  // status 도출 = approved-nodes-sql.ts 단일 진실원 공유 (CO-4 — 정상/graph/\n  // fallback 4 호출 측 동일 코어). LIKE 후보 한정은 status 코어 뒤 AND 결합\n  // (교환법칙 → 원 SQL 결과 집합 불변). status 코어엔 `?`0개 → bind 순서\n  // (likePattern×2) 불변. LIMIT 은 코어 외부에서 부착.\n  const sql =`${buildApprovedNodesQuery({\n    projection:\n      'kn.id, kn.type, kn.name, kn.description, kn.page_ref, kn.truth_weight, kn.is_current_active',\n    candidateFilter: '(kn.name LIKE ? OR kn.description LIKE ?)',\n  })}\n    LIMIT ${KEYWORD_PER_TOKEN_LIMIT}`;\n  try {\n    const result = await db.prepare(sql).bind(likePattern, likePattern).all<KeywordMatchRow>();\n    return result.results ?? [];\n  } catch (err) {\n    // Pass 3 M2 정합 — public message 에 SQL keyword 비포함. cause 보존.\n    throw new UserSearchError('Stage 2 keyword fallback 실패', 'filter', err);\n  }\n}\n","numLines":215,"startLine":1,"totalLines":215}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3fdf028c-d90b-41bf-a922-d1af94151ca9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/soo/ClaudePro/ThePick","sessionId":"fd694556-a969-4aad-ae22-32945ab476eb","version":"2.1.201","gitBranch":"main","slug":"zazzy-dancing-hellman"}

Worker 번들 미포함.\n9\t *\n10\t * ★ LOCAL_SMOKE 는 본 CLI 가 아니라 vitest 가 소유한다:\n11\t *   `apps/api/src/eval/__tests__/measure-runner.test.ts` 가 in-process Hono\n12\t *   + sqlite + stub Vectorize 로 합성 픽스처 end-to-end 를 **결정적·CI 게이트**\n13\t *   로 검증(G-6a-1/3). CLI 가 test helper(`__tests__/helpers`, C-CODE-2\n14\t *   no-restricted-imports)를 import 하지 않도록 remote 전용화 — 4-Pass\n15\t *   Pass3/4 M-1(lint 차단) + Pass1 M-1/M-2(스코프 외·취약 가드) 동시 해소.\n16\t *\n17\t * 사용 (진산 Cloudflare 인증 세션):\n18\t *   THEPICK_API_BASE=https://<배포 Worker> \\\n19\t *     pnpm tsx scripts/measure-s5-6-multihop-accuracy.ts \\\n20\t *       --golden <eval.json> [--limit N] [--out <dir>]\n21\t *   → 실 /api/search/graph 측정. REMOTE_G_S5. golden 파일·env 미충족 시\n22\t *     비측정 종료 (수치 fabricate 차단 — CLAUDE.md RULE #4/#5 / plan §3).\n23\t *     실 평가셋 golden 은 인증 세션이 remote D1 에서 추출한 산출물.\n24\t *\n25\t * 보안 (plan §4 G-6a-5): baseURL/자격증명은 **env 만** (인자/repo 평문 0).\n26\t */\n27\t\n28\timport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';\n29\timport { join, dirname } from 'node:path';\n30\timport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\n31\timport { EXAM_IDS } from '@thepick/shared';\n32\timport {\n33\t  parseRelatedNodes,\n34\t  scoreQuestion,\n35\t  aggregate,\n36\t  formatReportMarkdown,\n37\t  assertRemoteMeasurementInputs,\n38\t  type EvalGoldenItem,\n39\t  type GraphSearchResponseShape,\n40\t  type PerQuestionResult,\n41\t} from '../apps/api/src/eval/multihop-accuracy.js';\n42\t\n43\tconst HERE = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\n44\t\n45\t/** golden 파일 (진산 인증 세션이 remote D1 에서 추출). */\n46\tinterface GoldenFile {\n47\t  examId?: string;\n48\t  items: Array<{ questionId: string; content: string; relatedNodesRaw: string | null }>;\n49\t  coverageNote?: string;\n50\t}\n51\t\n52\tinterface Args {\n53\t  goldenPath: string | null;\n54\t  outDir: string;\n55\t  limit: number | null;\n56\t  /** graph-walk maxDepth (route 계약 1..MAX_ALLOWED_DEPTH=4). null = 미주입(엔진 기본). */\n57\t  maxDepth: number | null;\n58\t  /** 디버그 측정 모드 — route debug:true (query ≤2000 우회 + expandedNodes surface). G-WS4 ③④ 배선. */\n59\t  debug: boolean;\n60\t  /** Phase 1-D 비교군 — mode='lexical' 시 route 에 mode+debug:true 강제 주입 (plan rev3 §2-4). null = 미주입(graph byte-동치). */\n61\t  mode: 'lexical' | null;\n62\t  /** lexical ε (감도 스윕 M-5). null = 미주입(route 기본 0.03). */\n63\t  eps: number | null;\n64\t}\n65\t\n66\t/**\n67\t * route 계약 상한 사본 (graph-search-route.ts:86 MAX_ALLOWED_DEPTH=4) — 조기 fail-loud 용.\n68\t * ★ 권위 게이트는 route 자신(>4 면 400 거부)이므로 본 상수가 desync 돼도 측정은 안전 거부.\n69\t *   route ceiling 변경 시 본 상수 동반 갱신(별 패키지라 자동 추종 없음 — known MINOR, 검증 wf_f5b13834).\n70\t */\n71\tconst MAX_DEPTH_CEILING = 4;\n72\t\n73\t/**\n74\t * 인자 파싱. `--limit` 은 silent 흡수 금지 (4-Pass Pass1 M-3) — 양의 정수\n75\t * 아니면 throw (0/음수/NaN 이 전량·음수 slice 로 모집단을 무음 왜곡 차단).\n76\t */\n77\tfunction parseArgs(argv: string[]): Args {\n78\t  let goldenPath: string | null = null;\n79\t  let limit: number | null = null;\n80\t  let maxDepth: number | null = null;\n81\t  let debug = false;\n82\t  let mode: 'lexical' | null = null;\n83\t  let eps: number | null = null;\n84\t  let outDir = join(HERE, '..', 'docs', 'plans', 's5-6-measurements');\n85\t  for (let i = 2; i < argv.length; i++) {\n86\t    const a = argv[i];\n87\t    if (a === '--golden') goldenPath = argv[++i] ?? null;\n88\t    else if (a === '--debug') debug = true;\n89\t    else if (a === '--out') outDir = argv[++i] ?? outDir;\n90\t    else if (a === '--limit') {\n91\t      const rawLimit = argv[++i] ?? '').trim();\n if (!/^[0-9]+$/.test(rawDepth)) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `--maxDepth 는 1..${MAX_DEPTH_CEILING} 정수여야 함 (소수·비정수 거부, 받은 값: ${JSON.stringify(rawDepth)})`,\n        );\n      }\n      const n = Number.parseInt(rawDepth, 10);\n      if (n < 1 || n > MAX_DEPTH_CEILING) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `--maxDepth 는 1..${MAX_DEPTH_CEILING} 정수여야 함 (route 계약, 받은 값: ${JSON.stringify(rawDepth)})`,\n        );\n      }\n      maxDepth = n;\n    } else if (a === '--mode') {\n      const rawMode = (argv[++i] ?? '').trim();\n      if (rawMode !== 'lexical') {\n        throw new Error(\n          `--mode 는 'lexical' 만 허용 (graph = 미지정 기본, 받은 값: ${JSON.stringify(rawMode)})`,\n        );\n      }\n      mode = 'lexical';\n    } else if (a === '--eps') {\n      const rawEps = (argv[++i] ?? '').trim();\n      const n = Number.parseFloat(rawEps);\n      if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 0.1) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `--eps 는 0..0.1 실수여야 함 (route 계약, 받은 값: ${JSON.stringify(rawEps)})`,\n );\n }\n eps = n;\n } else if (a === '--local') {\n throw new Error(\n '--local 은 본 CLI 가 제공하지 않는다. LOCAL_SMOKE 는 vitest 소유 — ' +\n 'pnpm --filter @thepick/api test src/eval (G-6a-1/3 결정적 게이트).',\n );\n }\n }\n return { goldenPath, outDir, limit, maxDepth, debug, mode, eps };\n}\n\n/\*\*\n _ REMOTE fetch 재시도 — ADR-008 800ms Vectorize 하드 타임아웃(user-search.ts:42)이\n _ 간헐적으로 phase=timeout(504)을 던지는 것을 흡수(2026-06-05 실측: 쿼리 무관 ~1/5 산발).\n _ 측정 무결성 보존 규칙:\n _ - 5xx(간헐 infra timeout) = 재시도 — 결과는 결정적(성공 시 동일 top-K, 지연만 변동).\n _ - 4xx(validation·계약 등 결정적) = 즉시 fail-loud (재시도 무의미).\n _ - 재시도 소진 = fail-loud (빈결과 삼킴 금지). 각 재시도는 stderr 로깅(무음 아님).\n _ ★ fabricate 아님: 재시도는 flaky 지연만 넘길 뿐, 응답 본문/채점 로직은 불변.\n _/\nconst REMOTE_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 4;\nasync function fetchGraphWithRetry(\n url: string,\n requestBody: { examId: string; query: string; topK: number; maxDepth?: number; debug?: boolean },\n questionId: string,\n): Promise<GraphSearchResponseShape> {\n let lastStatus = 0;\n for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= REMOTE_MAX_ATTEMPTS; attempt++) {\n const r = await fetch(url, {\n method: 'POST',\n headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n body: JSON.stringify(requestBody),\n });\n if (r.ok) return (await r.json()) as GraphSearchResponseShape;\n lastStatus = r.status;\n // 4xx = 결정적(쿼리/계약 문제) → 재시도 무의미, 즉시 fail-loud.\n if (r.status >= 400 && r.status < 500) {\n throw new Error(`remote graph search ${r.status} for ${questionId} (non-retryable 4xx)`);\n }\n // 5xx = 간헐적 infra(ADR-008 800ms timeout 등) → backoff 후 재시도.\n if (attempt < REMOTE_MAX_ATTEMPTS) {\n const backoffMs = 600 _ attempt;\n process.stderr.write(\n `[retry ${attempt}/${REMOTE_MAX_ATTEMPTS - 1}] ${questionId}: HTTP ${r.status} → backoff ${backoffMs}ms\\n`,\n );\n await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, backoffMs));\n }\n }\n // 재시도 소진 → fail-loud (측정 무신뢰 차단).\n throw new Error(\n `remote graph search ${lastStatus} for ${questionId} after ${REMOTE_MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts (transient timeout exhausted)`,\n );\n}\n\nasync function runRemote(\n goldenPath: string | null,\n limit: number | null,\n maxDepth: number | null,\n debug: boolean,\n mode: 'lexical' | null,\n eps: number | null,\n): Promise<{ per: PerQuestionResult[]; golden: EvalGoldenItem[]; coverage: string }> {\n // 사전조건 = 순수 코어 단일 진실원 (G-6a-5 — 게이트 정책 drift 0).\n const { apiBase, goldenPath: gp } = assertRemoteMeasurementInputs(\n process.env.THEPICK*API_BASE,\n goldenPath,\n );\n const goldenFile = JSON.parse(readFileSync(gp, 'utf-8')) as GoldenFile;\n const examId = goldenFile.examId ?? EXAM_IDS.SON_HAE_PYEONG_GA_SA;\n const itemsRaw = limit !== null ? goldenFile.items.slice(0, limit) : goldenFile.items;\n\n const per: PerQuestionResult[] = [];\n const golden: EvalGoldenItem[] = [];\n for (const it of itemsRaw) {\n const parsed = parseRelatedNodes(it.relatedNodesRaw);\n // remote: expected 의 approved 교집합은 golden 추출 SQL 이 이미 적용\n // (인증 세션 책임). 본 runner 는 추출 결과를 그대로 채점.\n const item: EvalGoldenItem = {\n questionId: it.questionId,\n content: it.content,\n expected: parsed.ids,\n relatedRawCount: parsed.ids.length,\n relatedMalformed: parsed.malformed,\n };\n golden.push(item);\n // maxDepth 미주입 시 키 자체를 빼서 route 기본(엔진 default) 경로 보존 —\n // 원 측정(2026-06-01)과 byte-동치. 주입 시에만 명시 (maxDepth=1 재측정).\n const requestBody: {\n examId: string;\n query: string;\n topK: number;\n maxDepth?: number;\n debug?: boolean;\n mode?: 'lexical';\n eps?: number;\n } = {\n examId,\n query: it.content,\n topK: 5,\n };\n if (maxDepth !== null) requestBody.maxDepth = maxDepth;\n // 디버그 측정(G-WS4 ③④) — 미지정 시 키 자체를 빼서 원 측정과 byte-동치 유지.\n if (debug) requestBody.debug = true;\n // Phase 1-D lexical 비교군 (plan rev3 §2-4) — route 계약상 debug 필수 동반.\n if (mode === 'lexical') {\n requestBody.mode = 'lexical';\n requestBody.debug = true;\n if (eps !== null) requestBody.eps = eps;\n }\n // 간헐적 ADR-008 800ms timeout(504) 흡수 후 fail-loud — 채점 본문 불변.\n const body = await fetchGraphWithRetry(\n `${apiBase.replace(/\\/$/, '')}/api/search/graph`,\n requestBody,\n it.questionId,\n );\n per.push(scoreQuestion(item, body));\n }\n return {\n per,\n golden,\n // 측정 provenance: maxDepth 를 coverage 에 각인 (두 depth 리포트가 동일해 보이는 혼동 차단).\n // 'engine-default' 의 resolved 값은 **원격 배포 버전**에 따름 — 코드 기본은\n // 2026-06-11 결재 #6 부터 1 (~06-10 측정분은 2). 리뷰 S8 m-1: 라벨만으로 동일시 금지.\n coverage:\n (goldenFile.coverageNote ?? `REMOTE: ${itemsRaw.length} questions measured`) +\n ` | maxDepth=${maxDepth ?? 'engine-default(remote-resolved; code-default 1 since 2026-06-11)'}` +\n (mode === 'lexical'\n ? ` | mode=lexical eps=${eps ?? 0.03} — pool-rerank(주입 없음): onlyRecovery 는 pool 내 rank(topK+1..10] 진입 경로(D안 개선 기전·부모 게이트 충족 경로)이며 graph 의 외부-주입 회수와 의미 상이. 실측 0 = 음성 신호(rev3 C-N1)`\n : ''),\n };\n}\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n const { goldenPath, outDir, limit, maxDepth, debug, mode, eps } = parseArgs(process.argv);\n const { per, golden, coverage } = await runRemote(goldenPath, limit, maxDepth, debug, mode, eps);\n\n const report = aggregate(per, golden, coverage);\n const generatedAt = new Date().toISOString();\n const md = formatReportMarkdown(report, 'REMOTE_G_S5', generatedAt);\n\n mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });\n const stamp = generatedAt.replace(/[:.]/g, '').replace('T', '-').slice(0, 15);\n const base = join(\n outDir,\n `s5-6-remote-${mode === 'lexical' ? `lexical-eps${(eps ?? 0.03).toString().replace('.', '*')}` : 'g-s5'}-${stamp}`,\n );\n writeFileSync(`${base}.json`, JSON.stringify({ generatedAt, report }, null, 2));\n writeFileSync(`${base}.md`, md);\n // 성공만 조용히 — 산출물 경로 + 사실(수치 해석/판정은 진산, AI 자기채점 금지).\n process.stdout.write(`${md}\\n\\n[written] ${base}.md / ${base}.json\\n[mode] REMOTE_G_S5\\n`);\n}\n\n// CLI 전용 (어떤 모듈도 본 runner 를 import 하지 않음 — vitest 는 순수 코어\n// /helper 를 직접 사용). 따라서 무조건 실행 (취약한 argv 가드 불요 —\n// 4-Pass Pass1 M-2 해소: tsx/symlink 경로 불일치 no-op 위험 제거).\nvoid main().catch((err: unknown) => {\n process.stderr.write(`[FAIL] ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}\\n`);\n process.exitCode = 1;\n});\n","numLines":289,"startLine":1,"totalLines":289}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5c71f0b7-ef6c-416f-9080-a0b333ff73b6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/soo/ClaudePro/ThePick","sessionId":"fd694556-a969-4aad-ae22-32945ab476eb","version":"2.1.201","gitBranch":"main","slug":"zazzy-dancing-hellman"}

800ms hard timeout (AbortSignal.timeout)\n10\t * - 1 retry on network error\n11\t * - top-1 similarity < 0.60 → `gracefulDegradation: true` 플래그 (Multi-Path Fallback 진입 신호)\n12\t *\n13\t * Hard Rule 16/17 zero-cost:\n14\t * - 첫 인자 examId: ExamId 강제\n15\t * - EXAM_IDS 경유 100%, 리터럴 0건\n16\t *\n17\t * 비스코프 (별도 step carry-over):\n18\t * - Multi-Path Fallback (Rule 18) — 본 step 은 graceful 플래그만 응답\n19\t * - Concurrent Execution (Rule 23) — 별도 step\n20\t * - user_session 인증 + rate-limit\n21\t *\n22\t * 근거:\n23\t * - docs/architecture/SEARCH_PIPELINE.md v2.1 §2~§4\n24\t * - docs/adr/ADR-008-graceful-degradation.md\n25\t * - docs/adr/ADR-012-three-stage-hybrid-search.md\n26\t * - docs/adr/ADR-013-materialized-active-view.md\n27\t * - docs/plans/phase2a-user-search-route.plan.md §3.1\n28\t \*/\n29\t\n30\timport { TRUTH_WEIGHTS, type ExamId, type NodeType } from '@thepick/shared';\n31\timport type { AiBinding, VectorizeBinding } from '../vectorize/upserter.js';\n32\timport { parsePageRefToInt } from '../vectorize/page-ref.js';\n33\timport { buildApprovedNodesQuery } from './approved-nodes-sql.js';\n34\t\n35\t/** Stage 1 Vector Recall similarity 임계 (ADR-008). */\n36\texport const STAGE1_VECTOR_RECALL_MIN_SIMILARITY = 0.6;\n37\t\n38\t/\*\* ADR-008 graceful degradation 임계 (top-1 score < 0.60 → flag true). */\n39\texport const ADR_008_GRACEFUL_THRESHOLD = 0.6;\n40\t\n41\t/** ADR-008 Vectorize.query timeout (ms). \*/\n42\texport const ADR_008_TIMEOUT_MS = 800;\n43\t\n44\t/** ADR-008 retry count (network blip 대응). */\n45\texport const ADR_008_RETRY_COUNT = 1;\n46\t\n47\t/\*\* Stage 1 Vector Recall top-K (Stage 2/3 input candidate). */\n48\texport const STAGE1_TOP_K = 20;\n49\t\n50\t/**\n51\t * Stage 1 결과에서 client-side 제외할 표 벡터 id prefix — 결재 #13 (b) /\n52\t * ADR-047 (2026-07-02 진산 결재).\n53\t *\n54\t * production `thepick-embeddings` 인덱스에는 knowledge_nodes 794 외에 표 벡터\n55\t * 433(TBL- 20 / TROW-·TCOL- 167 / TCELL- 246 — ADR-032 Table-as-Micro-KG)이\n56\t * 동거한다. Stage 1 filter 는 `{ exam_id }` 단독 → 표 벡터가 top-20 후보\n57\t * 슬롯을 잠식하고, Stage 2 `fetchApprovedNodes` 는 knowledge_nodes 한정이라\n58\t * 표 벡터는 슬롯만 소모 후 **무음 탈락** + 표 벡터가 top-1 이면 top1Score/\n59\t * gracefulDegradation 판정까지 왜곡(제공 불가 후보가 graceful 억제)한다.\n60\t *\n61\t * 서버측 제외가 정공법이나 불가: knowledge_node 벡터의 node_type metadata 는\n62\t * 'knowledge_node' 고정값이 아닌 실 노드 type 12종(vectorize/routes.ts:367)\n63\t * → equality 단독으론 \"표 제외\" 표현 불가, `$nin`/`$ne` 는 V2 binding 미작동\n64\t * 실측(Session 061 — 아래 topic-cluster-router.ts STAGE3 선례 주석 참조).\n65\t \* 따라서 client-side prefix post-filter 채택. **한계(ADR-047 §D-3 명기)**:\n66\t * 슬롯 회복은 부분적 — top-20 회수 자체엔 표 벡터가 여전히 참여하며 제거분\n67\t * 만큼 knowledge_node 후보가 보충되진 않는다. 완전 회복은 표 전용 인덱스\n68\t * 분리(카드 #13 (c)) 또는 V2 filter 연산자 정상화 후 서버측 제외(Year 2\n69\t * carry-over) 몫. supersedes 채널 단일화 마이그는 \"첫 표 개정 전\" 게이트로\n70\t * 이연 (ADR-047 §D-2).\n71\t */\n72\texport const STAGE1_TABLE_VECTOR_EXCLUDE_PREFIXES = ['TBL-', 'TROW-', 'TCOL-', 'TCELL-'] as const;\n73\t\n74\t/**\n75\t * 표 벡터 id 판별 (ADR-047 §D-3) — Stage 1 잠식 차단 단일 판별원.\n76\t * TC-(topic_clusters)는 본 필터 범위 밖 — 카드 #13 은 표 벡터 433 한정\n77\t * (동일 잠식 기전이나 별도 처분, ADR-047 §결과 ⚠️).\n78\t */\n79\texport function isTableVectorId(id: string): boolean {\n80\t return STAGE1_TABLE_VECTOR_EXCLUDE_PREFIXES.some((prefix) => id.startsWith(prefix));\n81\t}\n82\t\n83\t/** 사용자 응답 default top-K (SEARCH_PIPELINE.md §4). \*/\n84\texport const DEFAULT_RESULT_TOP_K = 3;\n85\t\n86\t/** 사용자 응답 max top-K. */\n87\texport const MAX_RESULT_TOP_K = 10;\n88\t\n89\t/\*\*\n90\t * Vectorize metadata filter value 타입.\n91\t *\n92\t * Cloudflare Vectorize V2 spec 은 `$eq` / `$ne` / `$in` / `$nin` 객체 형식\n93\t * operator 를 명시하지만, Session 061 staging e2e 실측 결과 V2 binding 은 객체\n94\t * 형식 적용 시 0건 반환 (binding spec mismatch 또는 미배포 — Cloudflare changelog\n95\t * 모니터링 carry-over).\n96\t *\n97\t * 따라서 Year 1 운영 코드는 단순 `string | number | boolean` equality filter 만\n98\t * 사용 + client-side post-filter (예: topic-cluster-router.ts:STAGE3_NODE_ID_EXCLUDE_PREFIXES)\n99\t * 로 cross-pollution 차단.\n100\t *\n101\t * union 의 operator 분기 (`$eq`/`$ne`/`$in`/`$nin`) 는 Year 2 carry-over — V2\n102\t * binding 정상화 검증 후 활성화 예정 (4-Pass M2-2 / M4-C2 carry-over, ADR-004 §3\n103\t * Addendum 영속 의무).\n104\t */\n105\texport type VectorizeFilterValue =\n106\t | string\n107\t | number\n108\t | boolean\n109\t | { readonly $eq?: string | number | boolean }\n110\t | { readonly $ne?: string | number | boolean }\n111\t | { readonly $in?: ReadonlyArray<string | number | boolean> }\n112\t | { readonly $nin?: ReadonlyArray<string | number | boolean> };\n113\t\n114\t/**\n115\t * Vectorize.query 인터페이스 (upserter.ts VectorizeBinding 확장 — query 메서드 추가).\n116\t */\n117\texport interface VectorizeQueryBinding extends VectorizeBinding {\n118\t query(\n119\t values: ReadonlyArray<number>,\n120\t options: {\n121\t readonly topK: number;\n122\t readonly filter?: Record<string, VectorizeFilterValue>;\n123\t readonly returnMetadata?: 'none' | 'indexed' | 'all';\n124\t readonly returnValues?: boolean;\n125\t },\n126\t ): Promise<{\n127\t readonly count: number;\n128\t readonly matches: ReadonlyArray<{\n129\t readonly id: string;\n130\t readonly score: number;\n131\t readonly metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;\n132\t readonly values?: ReadonlyArray<number>;\n133\t }>;\n134\t }>;\n135\t}\n136\t\n137\t/** D1 인터페이스 (테스트 mock 호환 — 실 D1Database 와 구조 호환). */\n138\texport interface UserSearchD1 {\n139\t prepare(sql: string): {\n140\t bind(...params: unknown[]): {\n141\t all<T = Record<string, unknown>>(): Promise<{\n142\t readonly results?: ReadonlyArray<T>;\n143\t }>;\n144\t };\n145\t };\n146\t}\n147\t\n148\texport interface UserSearchDeps {\n149\t readonly ai: AiBinding;\n150\t readonly vectorize: VectorizeQueryBinding;\n151\t readonly db: UserSearchD1;\n152\t}\n153\t\n154\texport interface UserSearchHit {\n155\t readonly id: string;\n156\t readonly score: number;\n157\t readonly type: string;\n158\t readonly truthWeight: number;\n159\t readonly pageRef: number;\n160\t readonly name: string;\n161\t readonly description: string | null;\n162\t}\n163\t\n164\texport interface UserSearchResult {\n165\t readonly query: string;\n166\t readonly examId: ExamId;\n167\t readonly topK: number;\n168\t readonly gracefulDegradation: boolean;\n169\t readonly top1Score: number;\n170\t readonly stage1Count: number;\n171\t readonly stage2Count: number;\n172\t readonly results: ReadonlyArray<UserSearchHit>;\n173\t}\n174\t\n175\texport class UserSearchError extends Error {\n176\t readonly phase: 'embed' | 'query' | 'filter' | 'timeout';\n177\t readonly statusCode: number;\n178\t constructor(message: string, phase: 'embed' | 'query' | 'filter' | 'timeout', cause?: unknown) {\n179\t super(message);\n180\t this.name = 'UserSearchError';\n181\t this.phase = phase;\n182\t this.statusCode = phase === 'timeout' ? 504 : 500;\n183\t if (cause !== undefined) (this as { cause?: unknown }).cause = cause;\n184\t }\n185\t}\n186\t\n187\t/\*\*\n188\t * `buildHit` 가 실제로 소비하는 최소 필드 — status/is_current_active 는 미사용.\n189\t *\n190\t * S5-5 CO6-1: graph-walk 확장 노드(`GraphWalkNode`)도 이 형태로 매핑되어\n191\t * `buildHit` 단일 진실원을 *공유*한다 (잉여 2차 fetchApprovedNodes 제거). 즉\n192\t * hit 구성(truth_weight 도출·page_ref 정규화)이 정상 Stage 3 와 한 곳.\n193\t */\n194\texport interface NodeHitSource {\n195\t readonly id: string;\n196\t readonly type: string;\n197\t readonly name: string;\n198\t readonly description: string | null;\n199\t readonly page_ref: string | null;\n200\t readonly truth_weight: number;\n201\t}\n202\t\n203\texport interface ApprovedNodeRow extends NodeHitSource {\n204\t readonly status: string;\n205\t readonly is_current_active: number;\n206\t}\n207\t\n208\t/\*\*\n209\t * bge-m3 임베딩 단독 호출 (Stage 1 + Multi-Path Fallback Stage 3 재사용).\n210\t *\n211\t * Pass 2 MAJ-1 흡수 (Session 059): routes.ts 가 1회 호출 후 user-search +\n212\t * multi-path-fallback 양쪽에 전달 — request 당 bge-m3 호출 1회 보장 (CPU/비용 절감).\n213\t *\n214\t * @throws {UserSearchError} embed 실패 (1 retry 후)\n215\t */\n216\texport async function embedQuery(ai: AiBinding, query: string): Promise<ReadonlyArray<number>> {\n217\t if (!query || query.trim() === '') {\n218\t throw new UserSearchError('query must be non-empty', 'embed');\n219\t }\n220\t const aiResp = await runWithRetry(() => ai.run('@cf/baai/bge-m3', { text: [query] }), 'embed');\n221\t const queryVector = aiResp.data[0];\n222\t if (!Array.isArray(queryVector) || queryVector.length === 0) {\n223\t throw new UserSearchError('bge-m3 응답 차원 부재', 'embed');\n224\t }\n225\t return queryVector;\n226\t}\n227\t\n228\t/**\n229\t * 학습자 자연어 질문 → 검증된 knowledge_nodes top-K + 출처.\n230\t *\n231\t * 처리 단계:\n232\t * 1. Stage 1 Vector Recall: bge-m3 임베딩 → Vectorize.query (top-K=20, exam_id 필터)\n233\t * 2. Stage 2 Graph Hard Filter: D1 IN clause + status='approved' + is_current_active=1\n234\t * 3. Stage 3 Truth Weight Re-rank: TRUTH_WEIGHTS 가중치 정렬 (동일 weight 내 vector 보존)\n235\t * 4. graceful degradation 플래그 응답 (top-1 < 0.60)\n236\t *\n237\t * @param opts.precomputedEmbedding bge-m3 임베딩이 호출자(routes.ts) 에서 이미 계산된\n238\t * 경우 재사용 (Multi-Path Fallback 와 공유). Pass 2 MAJ-1 흡수 (Session 059).\n239\t *\n240\t * @throws {UserSearchError} embed 실패 / query 실패 / D1 실패 / timeout (800ms)\n241\t \*/\n242\texport async function searchKnowledgeNodesForUser(\n243\t deps: UserSearchDeps,\n244\t examId: ExamId,\n245\t query: string,\n246\t topK: number = DEFAULT_RESULT_TOP_K,\n247\t opts?: { readonly precomputedEmbedding?: ReadonlyArray<number> },\n248\t): Promise<UserSearchResult> {\n249\t if (!examId || (examId as string).trim() === '') {\n250\t throw new UserSearchError('examId is required (Hard Rule 16 시험 경계 강제)', 'filter');\n251\t }\n252\t if (!query || query.trim() === '') {\n253\t throw new UserSearchError('query must be non-empty', 'embed');\n254\t }\n255\t const effectiveTopK = Math.min(Math.max(topK, 1), MAX_RESULT_TOP_K);\n256\t\n257\t // Stage 1.a: bge-m3 임베딩 — precomputed 있으면 재사용 (Pass 2 MAJ-1)\n258\t const queryVector =\n259\t opts?.precomputedEmbedding !== undefined\n260\t ? opts.precomputedEmbedding\n261\t : await embedQuery(deps.ai, query);\n262\t\n263\t // Stage 1.b: Vectorize.query (timeout + retry)\n264\t const rawMatches = await runVectorizeQueryWithTimeout(deps.vectorize, queryVector, examId);\n265\t\n266\t // ADR-047 §D-3 (결재 #13 (b)): 표 벡터(TBL-/TROW-/TCOL-/TCELL-) 잠식 차단 —\n267\t // Stage 2 knowledge_nodes 한정으로 어차피 무음 탈락할 후보를 회수 직후 제외해\n268\t // top1Score/graceful 판정·stage1Count 를 제공 가능 후보만으로 정화한다.\n269\t // 서버측 $nin 미작동(V2 binding 실측) → client-side prefix post-filter\n270\t // (STAGE1_TABLE_VECTOR_EXCLUDE_PREFIXES 주석의 부분 회복 한계 참조).\n271\t const vectorMatches = rawMatches.filter((m) => !isTableVectorId(m.id));\n272\t\n273\t if (vectorMatches.length === 0) {\n274\t return buildEmptyResult(query, examId, effectiveTopK, 0);\n275\t }\n276\t\n277\t // ADR-008: similarity ≥ 0.60 필터 (Stage 1 임계)\n278\t const stage1Filtered = vectorMatches.filter(\n279\t (m) => m.score >= STAGE1_VECTOR_RECALL_MIN_SIMILARITY,\n280\t );\n281\t const top1Score = vectorMatches[0]?.score ?? 0;\n282\t const gracefulDegradation = top1Score < ADR_008_GRACEFUL_THRESHOLD;\n283\t\n284\t if (stage1Filtered.length === 0) {\n285\t return {\n286\t query,\n287\t examId,\n288\t topK: effectiveTopK,\n289\t gracefulDegradation,\n290\t top1Score,\n291\t stage1Count: 0,\n292\t stage2Count: 0,\n293\t results: [],\n294\t };\n295\t }\n296\t\n297\t // Stage 2: Graph Hard Filter (D1)\n298\t const stage2Rows = await fetchApprovedNodes(\n299\t deps.db,\n300\t stage1Filtered.map((m) => m.id),\n301\t );\n302\t\n303\t if (stage2Rows.length === 0) {\n304\t // 모든 후보가 draft / is_current_active=0 → graceful 강제 (Multi-Path Fallback 진입 신호)\n305\t return {\n306\t query,\n307\t examId,\n308\t topK: effectiveTopK,\n309\t gracefulDegradation: true,\n310\t top1Score,\n311\t stage1Count: stage1Filtered.length,\n312\t stage2Count: 0,\n313\t results: [],\n314\t };\n315\t }\n316\t\n317\t // Stage 3: Truth Weight Re-rank (vector similarity preserved within same weight)\n318\t const scoreById = new Map(stage1Filtered.map((m) => [m.id, m.score]));\n319\t const reranked = stage2Rows\n320\t .map((row) => buildHit(row, scoreById.get(row.id) ?? 0))\n321\t .sort(compareByTruthWeightThenScore);\n322\t\n323\t return {\n324\t query,\n325\t examId,\n326\t topK: effectiveTopK,\n327\t gracefulDegradation,\n328\t top1Score,\n329\t stage1Count: stage1Filtered.length,\n330\t stage2Count: stage2Rows.length,\n331\t results: reranked.slice(0, effectiveTopK),\n332\t };\n333\t}\n334\t\n335\tfunction buildEmptyResult(\n336\t query: string,\n337\t examId: ExamId,\n338\t topK: number,\n339\t top1Score: number,\n340\t): UserSearchResult {\n341\t return {\n342\t query,\n343\t examId,\n344\t topK,\n345\t gracefulDegradation: true,\n346\t top1Score,\n347\t stage1Count: 0,\n348\t stage2Count: 0,\n349\t results: [],\n350\t };\n351\t}\n352\t\n353\t/**\n354\t * Stage 3 \"Truth Weight Re-rank\" 비교자 — **단일 진실원 (CO-3)**.\n355\t *\n356\t * truth_weight 내림차순(LAW=10 > FORMULA=8 > … > TERM=3), 동일 weight 내\n357\t * vector score 보존(내림차순). graph-walk 경로(`/api/search/graph`)도 본\n358\t * 비교자를 *공유*한다 — graph-walk 는 최종 랭킹을 정하지 않으며(hop-distance\n359\t * 로 recall bound 만 책임).\n360\t *\n361\t * **단일 진실원 범위 (정밀):** \"정상 Stage 3 + graph-walk 경로\" 의 truth_weight\n362\t * 최종 정렬 유일 출처가 본 함수다. multi-path-fallback 의 topic-cluster\n363\t * 라우터(`topic-cluster-router.ts` ranked sort)는 *별개 의미 맥락*으로\n364\t * `truth_weight → score → name.localeCompare` 3-key 결정적 정렬을 의도적으로\n365\t * 사용한다(폴백 결과 안정 정렬용 name tiebreak — drift 아님, 설계 차이).\n366\t * 정상/graph 경로에 2번째 truth_weight 정책 생성 금지가 본 주석의 계약이다\n367\t * (graph-walk-s5-integration.plan.md §1 CO-3 / SEARCH_PIPELINE.md §2 /\n368\t * ADR-012 §Decision Stage 3).\n369\t */\n370\texport function compareByTruthWeightThenScore(a: UserSearchHit, b: UserSearchHit): number {\n371\t if (a.truthWeight !== b.truthWeight) return b.truthWeight - a.truthWeight;\n372\t return b.score - a.score;\n373\t}\n374\t\n375\texport function buildHit(row: NodeHitSource, score: number): UserSearchHit {\n376\t const nodeType = row.type as NodeType;\n377\t // S5-5 CO6-4(e): truth_weight 정렬 안전 가드. TRUTH_WEIGHTS 미등록 type +\n378\t // row.truth_weight 가 NULL/NaN(D1 REAL 결측·graph 확장이 노출 확대) 이면\n379\t // `compareByTruthWeightThenScore` 의 `!==`/뺄셈이 NaN 으로 정렬 불능 →\n380\t // baseline 정답률 왜곡. `?? 0` 만으로 NaN 미차단이라 Number.isFinite 로 0 폴백.\n381\t const rawTruthWeight = TRUTH_WEIGHTS[nodeType] ?? row.truth_weight;\n382\t const truthWeight = Number.isFinite(rawTruthWeight) ? rawTruthWeight : 0;\n383\t return {\n384\t id: row.id,\n385\t score,\n386\t type: row.type,\n387\t truthWeight,\n388\t pageRef: parsePageRefToInt(row.page_ref).value,\n389\t name: row.name,\n390\t description: row.description,\n391\t };\n392\t}\n393\t\n394\t/\**\n395\t * Vectorize.query + Promise.race timeout(800ms) + 1 retry on network error.\n396\t *\n397\t * timeout 구현 = Promise.race + setTimeout (clearTimeout cleanup 의무 — 5th C-1 흡수, Session 058).\n398\t * AbortSignal.timeout 은 vectorize binding 이 직접 지원하지 않아 race 패턴 사용.\n399\t * timeout 초과 시 UserSearchError(phase=timeout) throw.\n400\t *\n401\t * retry 정책 (Pass 4 M1 흡수):\n402\t * - network error 1회 재시도, 300ms backoff\n403\t * - timeout 은 retry 안 함 (의도적 빠른 실패)\n404\t \*/\n405\tasync function runVectorizeQueryWithTimeout(\n406\t vectorize: VectorizeQueryBinding,\n407\t queryVector: ReadonlyArray<number>,\n408\t examId: ExamId,\n409\t): Promise<ReadonlyArray<{ id: string; score: number; metadata?: Record<string, unknown> }>> {\n410\t const filter = { exam_id: examId as string };\n411\t const options = {\n412\t topK: STAGE1_TOP_K,\n413\t filter,\n414\t returnMetadata: 'all' as const,\n415\t };\n416\t\n417\t const queryWithTimeout = async (): Promise<{\n418\t matches: ReadonlyArray<{ id: string; 800ms hard timeout (AbortSignal.timeout)\n * - 1 retry on network error\n * - top-1 similarity < 0.60 → `gracefulDegradation: true` 플래그 (Multi-Path Fallback 진입 신호)\n *\n * Hard Rule 16/17 zero-cost:\n * - 첫 인자 examId: ExamId 강제\n * - EXAM_IDS 경유 100%, 리터럴 0건\n *\n * 비스코프 (별도 step carry-over):\n * - Multi-Path Fallback (Rule 18) — 본 step 은 graceful 플래그만 응답\n * - Concurrent Execution (Rule 23) — 별도 step\n * -
